Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The stratigraphy variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 592 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 13 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_592(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 592. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^13(S, F) is trivial. QED.
